<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-81E9148A-2F48-4754-A14C-C49051FB1236'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Circle the number in the list below which is closest to the value $1$

$0.993   \quad 1.006  \quad 1.1  \quad 0.94  \quad 0.99$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's calculate the difference between the number and $1$.

| Number   |     Difference      |
|:--------:|:-------------------:|
|  $0.993$ | $1 - 0.993 = 0.007$ |
|  $1.006$ | $1.006 - 1 = 0.006$ |
|  $1.1$   | $1.1 - 1 = 0.1$     |
|  $0.94$  | $1 - 0.94 = 0.06$   |
|  $0.99$  | $1 - 0.99 = 0.01$   |

From the calculation above, the smallest difference is $0.006$.

Number closest in value $= 1.006$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.006$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's calculate the difference between the number and $1$.

| Number   |     Difference      |
|:--------:|:-------------------:|

|  $1.006$ | $1.006 - 1 = 0.006$ |
|  $1.1$   | $1.1 - 1 = 0.1$     |
|  $0.94$  | $1 - 0.94 = 0.06$   |
|  $0.99$  | $1 - 0.99 = 0.01$   |

From the calculation above, the smallest difference is $0.006$.

Number closest in value $= 1.006$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1.006$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Which number is multipled by $100$ to give $2907$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  2907 \div 100 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {2907} {100} \\\\
&=  29.07
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$29.07$

</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question question'>

Circle the three numbers in the list below which have a sum of $43$.

$5 \quad 8 \quad 11 \quad 14 \quad 21 \quad 31$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$8, 14, 21$

Let's verify it.

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  8 + 14 + 21 \\\\
&=  22 + 21 \\\\
&=  43
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8, 14, 21$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8, 14, 21$

Let's verify it.

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  8 + 14 + 21 \\\\
&=  22 + 21 \\\\
&=  43
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$4 \times \boxed{\phantom{1}} + 8 = 44$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times a + 8  &= 44 \\\\
4 a             &= 44 - 8 \\\\
4 a             &= 36 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {36} {4} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {9 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a               &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$9$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

a               &= \dfrac {36} {4} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {9 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a               &= 9
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3 \times (5 - a)    &= 12 \\\\
15 - 3 a            &= 12 \\\\
3 a                 &= 15 - 12 \\\\
3 a                 &= 3 \\\\
a                   &= 1
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1$

<div class='question subquestion'>

$40   \div  \ \boxed{\phantom{1}} = 4^2 \div 2$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
40 \div a         &= 4 ^ 2 \div 2 \\\\
\dfrac {40} {a}   &= 4 \times 4 \div 2 \\\\
\dfrac {40} {a}   &= \dfrac {4 \times 4} {2} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {40 \times 2} {4 \times 4} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {4 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2} {4 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {2} \times 5 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
a                 &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

a                 &= \dfrac {4 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2} {4 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {2} \times 5 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
a                 &= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class='question question'>

Summer has correctly used her calculator to work out that

$17 \times 538 = 9146$

Without doing any long multiplication or division, use Summer’s calculation to work out:

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$17 \times 540$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 17 \times 540 \\\\
&= 17 \times (538 + 2) \\\\
&= 17 \times 538 + 17 \times 2 \\\\
&= 9146 + 34 \\\\
&= 9180
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$9180$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 17 \times 540 \\\\
&= 17 \times (538 + 2) \\\\
&= 17 \times 538 + 17 \times 2 \\\\
&= 9146 + 34 \\\\
&= 9180
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$9180$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$27 \times 538$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 27 \times 538 \\\\
&= (10 + 17) \times 538 \\\\
&= 10 \times 538 + 17 \times 538 \\\\
&= 5380 + 9146 \\\\
&= 14526
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14526$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 27 \times 538 \\\\
&= (10 + 17) \times 538 \\\\
&= 10 \times 538 + 17 \times 538 \\\\
&= 5380 + 9146 \\\\
&= 14526
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$9146 \div 34$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 9146 \div 34 \\\\
&= \dfrac {9146} {34} \\\\
&= \dfrac {17 \times 538} {34} \\\\
&= \dfrac {17 \times 2 \times 269} {17 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {17} \times \cancel {2} \times 269} {\cancel {17} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 269
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$269$

</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac {17 \times 538} {34} \\\\
&= \dfrac {17 \times 2 \times 269} {17 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {17} \times \cancel {2} \times 269} {\cancel {17} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 269
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8CA178E1-3E53-4A12-A620-3A797155DEEC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Given that $7.84 \times 6 = 46.8$, work out the value of $7.84 \times 18$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>



$
\begin{aligned}
&=7.84 \times 18 \\\\
&=7.84 \times 6 \times 3 \\\\
&=46.8 \times 3\\\\
&=140.4     
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$140.4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=7.84 \times 18 \\\\
&=7.84 \times 6 \times 3 \\\\
&=46.8 \times 3\\\\
&=140.4     
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

Sam has the six number cards shown below.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


The cards can be placed together to form different numbers.

For example, using just five of the cards, the largest $5$-digit number that can be made is $87652$.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Which number is $1000$ times smaller than $700$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 700 \times \dfrac{1}{1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {700} {1000} \\\\
&= 0.700
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.700$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

\begin{aligned}
&= 700 \times \dfrac{1}{1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {700} {1000} \\\\
&= 0.700
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

$17 + \boxed{\phantom{11}} = 23 \times 2$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
17 + a  &= 23 \times 2 \\\\
17 + a  &= 46 \\\\
a       &= 46 - 17 \\\\
a       &= 29 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$29$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
17 + a  &= 23 \times 2 \\\\
17 + a  &= 46 \\\\
a       &= 46 - 17 \\\\
a       &= 29 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$9 + (5 \times 7) = 47 - \boxed{\phantom{11}}$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
9 + (5 \times 7)  &= 47 - a \\\\
9 + 35            &= 47 - a \\\\
44                &= 47 - a\\\\
a                &= 47 - 44 \\\\
a                 &= 3 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
9 + (5 \times 7)  &= 47 - a \\\\
9 + 35            &= 47 - a \\\\
44                &= 47 - a\\\\
a                &= 47 - 44 \\\\
a                 &= 3 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$\boxed{\phantom{11}}  \ \times 16 = 36 \div 9$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 16  &= 36 \div 9 \\\\
a \times 16  &= \dfrac {36} {9} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {36} {9 \times 16} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {9 \times 4} {9 \times 4 \times 4} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {\cancel {9} \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {9} \times 4 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {1} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 16  &= 36 \div 9 \\\\
a \times 16  &= \dfrac {36} {9} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {36} {9 \times 16} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {9 \times 4} {9 \times 4 \times 4} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {\cancel {9} \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {9} \times 4 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
a            &= \dfrac {1} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {4}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

&= \dfrac {75} {10} \\\\
&= 7.5
\end {aligned}
$

&= \dfrac {900} {12 \times 10} \\\\

&= 7.5
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class='question question'>

Write in numerals, the number that is *three hundred* less than *fourteen thousand and fifty*.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 14050 - 300 \\\\
&= 13750
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13750$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 14050 - 300 \\\\
&= 13750
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-303AB058-2EC9-4DEB-9A11-AE3828D6B5BC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write the missing sign $( = , <$ or $>)$ in the box.

$16 \times 7  \ \boxed{\phantom{11}} \ 17 \times 6$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
16 \times 7  &= 112 \\\\
17 \times 6  &= 102 \\\\
16 \times 7  \quad &\boxed{\green
{>}} \quad 17 \times 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$16 \times 7  \ \boxed{>} \ 17 \times 6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
16 \times 7  &= 112 \\\\
17 \times 6  &= 102 \\\\
16 \times 7  \quad &\boxed{\rtq_katex_color_answer{>}} \quad 17 \times 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
